// Drum Patterns (transcribed from the 1987 book "260 Drum Machine Patterns" by Rene-Pierre Bardet)
// some clean up was done by @rommix0 so CY are now two separate instruments (ride and crash)
// and empty TMB is added to every single pattern that didn't have one
//
// author: Anthony C. Bartman (@rommix0)
// date:   5/30/2026
//
// LEGENDS:
// 		AC		accent
// 		CY.R	ride cymbal
// 		CY.C	crash cymbal
// 		CH		closed hi-hat
// 		OH		open hi-hat
// 		HT		high tom
// 		MT		mid tom
// 		LT		low tom
// 		BD		bass drum
// 		SD		snare drum
// 		RS		rim shot
// 		CPS		claps
// 		CB		cowbell
// 		TMB		tambourine

TANGO:

AC		-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	#	-
CY.R	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-	-
